# Proposed OEIS comments: A296798 to A299047

One section per OEIS entry: the date the result was obtained, the paper that proves it, what the entry still records as unsettled, and the text proposed for posting on that entry.

**Nothing here has been posted.** See [../SUBMITTING.md](../SUBMITTING.md).

---

## A296798

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A296798>  
Result obtained: **31 August 2026**  
Paper: [9215](../papers/09001-09500/09215-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = a(n-1) + 3*a(n-2) + 9*a(n-3) - 4*a(n-4) - 12*a(n-5) - 16*a(n-6).
> Empirical g.f.: x*(1 + 2*x)*(2 + 2*x - 3*x^2 - 10*x^3 - 8*x^4) / ((1 - x^2 - 2*x^3)*(1 - x - 2*x^2 - 8*x^3)). - _Colin Barker_, Feb 25 2019

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 16 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 7. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. ```

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 4*a(n-1) +45*a(n-2) +198*a(n-3) +1120*a(n-4) -571*a(n-5) +949*a(n-6) -25951*a(n-7) +43907*a(n-8) -121981*a(n-9) +237707*a(n-10) -305033*a(n-11) +951497*a(n-12) -2179565*a(n-13) +1393985*a(n-14) +38155*a(n-15) +856624*a(n-16) +1417955*a(n-17) -2925138*a(n-18) +4386425*a(n-19) -3349236*a(n-20) +7904975*a(n-21) -936917*a(n-22) +6331851*a(n-23) -431305*a(n-24) -2123285*a(n-25) -1887476*a(n-26) -6000225*a(n-27) -5171754*a(n-28) -5025966*a(n-29) -3580724*a(n-30) +728778*a(n-31) +1200558*a(n-32) +668712*a(n-33) +511075*a(n-34) +1377776*a(n-35) +458144*a(n-36) +151448*a(n-37) -54584*a(n-38) -42912*a(n-39) -30464*a(n-40) +3840*a(n-41)

```

## A297698

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A297698>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [10679](../papers/10501-11000/10679-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 60 (see link above)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 83 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 60, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A298059

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A298059>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [10511](../papers/10501-11000/10511-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 71 (see link above)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 72 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 71, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 70 (see link above)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 72 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 70, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A298967

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A298967>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [254](../papers/00001-00500/00254-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 75 (see link above).

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 1024 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 75, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```
